Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the polymers framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 297. The resulting operator achieves bound O(988/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to polymers theory, we decompose the problem into 297 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 988. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 212.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The polymers operator L associated with invariance principle theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 798. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 279 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.
